[Galatean Outpost]
The dirt road curves around the crumbled outpost. Rotting wood pillars and planks, worn from ages of war and disuse, cave in on one another, giving the old outpost and sunken look. [Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. The area is illuminated.

You find yourself floating in a vast, pitch black void...

A slow, musing presence enters your consciousness. You can feel it gently reaching out, feeling, examining you. It speaks in a calm and slow voice...
           
"Hmm... yes, what do we have here? Another lost soul drifting on the tides of the abyss, are you? How strange indeed that so many are trapped here... perhaps another catastrophic war? Or maybe the wrath of a particularly neglected divinity... they are rather temperamental, you know. But let us focus on you... perhaps you had a name at one point? What was it that others addressed you as?"

"Gal Irrim... very well. I can feel that there is still some spark alive in you, and your place is not with the fallen, at least not yet. Come... let us bring you back towards the world of mortals, so that you may rejoin their ranks.

Though you cannot see anything in the darkness, you sense yourself rapidly moving, drawn by some invisible force. For how long, you cannot tell, but in a sudden flash you find yourself looking down upon a group of figures frozen in time.

The voice speaks, "Before you are the races of the mortal plane. Though in the past you may have belonged to one, you are free to choose whichever form you wish to return as. Each of these races is unique in their own regard. Some possess great strength, others great intellect. Choose wisely, as once you have chosen there is no reversing it."

[Southern Cross Road]
n
Neatly kept cobblestones lead north and south the cracks between them free of dirt and debris. The large black gate which allows entrance to the city from Travers towers above several trees to the south. To the north the road curves slightly then begins to straighten. The road both north and south is lined on either side by brass lanterns which hang close to the ground from dark walnut posts allowing for plenty of light even on the darkest nights. To the west and the east a neat but narrower road appears to parallel the tall city walls which dots the sky in the distance.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, south, east, north

[Southern Cross Road]
City guards pass by frequently as they make their rounds. To the west the roof of a large building can be seen peaking over a few trees. A small man made waterfall feeds a small pond which bubbles happily on the eastern edge of the road.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north


[Southern Cross Road]
Oak trees and Birch trees which house several types of wildlife line the east and west side of the street.  A few small but functional benches are scattered neatly amongst the trees to allow for rest and relaxation. Hanging from a metal pole on the east side of the road is a small black sign with white lettering that reads "This road maintained by the Urda Merchants Council"

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north


[Southern Cross Road]
The cobblestone street displays wear in some areas due to the frequent traffic both adventurer and merchant who travel from the city of Travers. A large wooden bulletin with remnants of old parchment paper, makes its home on the western side of the road. Frequently new postings can be found advertising services, festivals and other city happenings.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

w

[Western Cross Road]
Wagon marks mar the uneven cobbled road from so much use. Shops spring up on either side of the road, their owners gesturing for patrons to come in and try their new wares. A large Galirrim, towering above the other passersby, lumbers towards the town square in shreds of clothing. Other pedestrians make way for the Galirrim and go about their business, never giving him a second thought. The diversity of races walking down the road is astounding.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Tuva the Galirrim, 1st wolf cub
Obvious exits are: west, south, east

e

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Tuva the Galirrim, 1st wolf cub
In this room are: eastern monument, southwestern monument, northwestern monument, southern monument, large wooden bulletin board, southeastern monument, northern monument, western monument, northeastern monument
Obvious exits are: west, southwest, south, east, north

n

[Northern Cross Road]
The bustle and activity of the city square echoes from the south, as well as the not-too-far-off noises of the Merchant Run. To the north, pedestrians amble on their way home up to the residential districts of the city. Town criers dash from place to place, as guards, soldiers, merchants, and inhabitants alike rush about on their business.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Travers Town Square]

Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Tuva the Galirrim, 1st city waste engineer
Obvious exits are: northwest, south, southeast, east, northeast, north, closed grate, open off-center wooden door

>


[Combat Lounge]
The wooden walls of this room are covered with posters and pictures of figures in various combat positions. They exhibit a wide range of possible tactics that can be used with various weapons. A grizzled veteran, looking quite bored, leans against one of the walls and seems to be only interested in making sure the trainees fighting does not spill into the rest of the guild. A couple of couches are spread around the room to provide rest for those seeking to catch their breath, but a large sign on the northern wall indicates anyone with serious injuries should report to the healer across the central hall. An archway leads outside to the penned-in training area that are available to practice combat skills.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Tuva the Galirrim
Obvious exits are: northwest, west, wooden ladder, massive steel portcullis, stone stairs

[Combat Training Area]

[Combat Training Area]
This far corner of the training area appears to be popular for the cowardly among the trainees to congregate in. A couple of them lean against the wall and are having a hard time catching their breath, a sure sign of being off-limits for attack. Nevertheless, inevitably one of the more aggressive students will stalk into the area, point his weapon accusingly at one of those on the wall, and the two run off, one giving chase to the other.

The area is illuminated.

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Tuva the Galirrim, 1st city waste engineer, 2nd city waste engineer
Obvious exits are: northwest, south, southeast, east, northeast, north, closed grate, open off-center wooden door

se

[Combat Lounge]
The wooden walls of this room are covered with posters and pictures of figures in various combat positions. They exhibit a wide range of possible tactics that can be used with various weapons. A grizzled veteran, looking quite bored, leans against one of the walls and seems to be only interested in making sure the trainees fighting does not spill into the rest of the guild. A couple of couches are spread around the room to provide rest for those seeking to catch their breath, but a large sign on the northern wall indicates anyone with serious injuries should report to the healer across the central hall. An archway leads outside to the penned-in training area that are available to practice combat skills.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Tuva the Galirrim
Obvious exits are: northwest, west, wooden ladder, massive steel portcullis, stone stairs

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

[North Central Hall]
The central hallway of the Heroes Guild is decorated with portraits and busts of heroes of the Old Kingdom.  The pictures fill up nearly every inch of available wall space with distinguished-looking Cyrwian men and women. Along the walls darkened bronze sconces illuminate the portraits. Each portrait bears a bronze plaque with a name etched upon it, but years of neglect have rendered them tarnished and unreadable. From the east, are the faint cries and shouts of pitched combat, and, as if on cue, another young cadet rushes in and immediately out, heading west to the healer's room.

The area is illuminated.

[Healer]
A single harried-looking woman rushes about the hectic room dealing with the multitude of injuries that seem to keep cropping up. She glances up for a moment to register your presence, then the next she seems to ignore you and moves to another bed in the room. Blue magic flows from her arms and heals a particularly rough scrape another trainee has received before she turns to the one behind her, attempting to reattatch a lost limb. She speaks in a low husky voice, "If you need my help, please speak up!! Otherwise, stop crowding the room and attend to your studies, I have too much to do!" Once she finishes, she mutters darkly about the damned authorities refusing her an aide.

The area is illuminated.

[The Lonely Moon: Stars Club, Foyer]
The doors open into a quaint foyer with heavily polished cherrywood floors. Elegantly carved ebonwood pillars rise from the floor at the corners where the walls, papered in murals of detailed depictions of the night sky and its constellations, come together. A dimly lit, diamond and platinum-dusted chandelier hangs from the darkened ceiling like a bundle of shooting stars. Further down the corridor from the foyer is a heavy oak door, studded with silver stars and a lone, crescent moon painted in the center. A burly duskwen guards the back door. A cheerily lit side room to the west houses a myriad of unique clothing on mannequins, teak hangers, and cedarwood shelves, as well as glass counters filled with sparkling jewels and baubles. The eastern archway from the foyer leads into a cramped post office, dominated by a large counter in front of a vast array of tiny envelope cabinets.

The area is illuminated.

[The Lonely Moon: Stars Club, Tavern]
Behind the heavy oak door, the dimly-lit room opens into a spacious tavern full of glass-topped, lacquered wooden tables and cushioned chairs. A beautifully polished bar, carved with star and tree shapes, is set against the northern wall. Behind the bar top, bottles of liquor, kegs of beer, and all manner of alcoholic beverage are lined up, tapped and waiting for patrons. High-backed stools are set against the bar, allowing patrons to mingle with one another. The cacophonous sounds of talking, laughing, and whispering fill the packed room. The hearty aroma of well-cooked food wafts from the kitchen entrance behind the bar. Servers and bartenders move swiftly back and forth from the tavern to the kitchen. A T-shaped stage is centered at the eastern wall, and though the barmaids and barkeeps continue to move quickly, refilling drinks or bringing food in and out from the kitchen behind the bar, a reverent silence spreads over the customers as entertainers take the stage. Portraits of various famous adventurers decorate the walls, yet the most pronounced portrait of the owner has been hung behind the bar.

The area is illuminated.
